### Round 25 — 2026-11-19

Source: Codex — codex gpt-6.2-astra medium — Phase 3 code peer review (stage-3.p1, peer round 2)  
Status: Closed (8 applied in leg cx7, verify-then-fix; verified 2026-11-19 by the composer's re-runs `ffl-selftest-24.txt` (0 mismatches; against `ffl-selftest-23.txt` only `seconds:` differs), `ffl-ruff-12.txt` (all checks passed), `ffl-map-4.txt` (exit 0, `MAP problems: 0`, `[written] map-4.md`, no `.part` file: 67 rows, 33 counted, every row's set, encounter, fact, predicate, class and counted cells equal to `map-3`; against `ffl-map-3.txt` only the renamed column header, the section title `Rows marked mixed or unmeasured (0)` and the `[written]` name differ; no row gains `S3(unmeasured)`), `ffl-audio-2.txt` (exit 0, errors 0; `docs-summary-2.md` differs from `docs-summary.md` only in its `seconds:` line, the 12 seed lines identical), and the refusal smokes `ffl-map-smoke-3.txt` and `ffl-audio-smoke-4.txt` (both exit 2, `needs --out in an existing folder`, no `no-such-folder` created); `map.md`, `map-2.md`, `map-3.md` and `docs-summary.md` unchanged (SHA-256 before and after))

Scope:

- job 0: checked all eleven round-24 fixes. Map 3 preserves all 67 row identities, classes and counted flags from map 2. All 29 wrong and wrongly-present rows now carry the text-silent flag. The S7 boundary toy falls through to S9. The seed, set-class and exact-two-takes checks each have reachable acceptance and refusal paths. The four corrected plan claims agree with the outputs and shipped R3 predicate. Duplicate input identities can still bypass two of the new guards.
- job 1: checked D5’s ordered tests against the classifier, shipped judgement logic and five encounter traces. `h2-04/f6` is S2: its sole segment, s13, is patient speech under a label with 9.080 seconds of partner speech and 8.248 seconds of patient speech. `syn-22/f2` is `unknown`: every fact token was deleted, but eight non-suspect words elsewhere on L4 aligned to s8–s9, so S6 correctly fails. S3 has a reachable case where the guard result is unavailable and the classifier reports `no`.
- job 2: checked the saved population against the baseline, `class_counts`, rule v6 and both 0.3.7 archives. Map 3 contains 67 distinct failures: 13 silent, 16 wrong, 13 wrongly present, 18 encounter-code rows and seven set breaches across six sets. The ranked counts sum to 67, with 33 counted. All seven per-set totals agree. The 12 archived silent seeds and the wrong `h6-09/f1` retain their archived states on take 1.
- job 3: checked the base and eight variants over 183 take-1 and 44 take-2 encounters, with suggestions and prose off. The D8 tables, zero level floors and all 12 text/audio seed lines agree with the saved outputs. Both key sets’ take tables agree, including their detail lists. On both takes A1 reaches `h2-04/f6`, F-credit reaches four seeds, and the rejected turn form reaches `h6-16/f5` beyond A1.
- job 4: checked both publish sites, the five refusal logs and the text defaults. The final pathname is linked only after the write closes, and an existing destination survives a competing publish. Ordinary exceptions run the sibling cleanup. Forced process termination during writing can leave an orphan `.part`, while the final pathname stays absent. The text run reports zero errors and 63 projections. Its log records generation; the byte comparison and smoke hashes are reported separately by the composer in the plan.
- job 5: checked the Phase 3 records and cx1–cx6 handoffs against the named outputs. The numerical results and seed IDs agree. The trace contradicts one causal claim about which piece of `h2-07` closed the follow-on chain. The map’s interpretation comment also retains the pre-round-24 origin-test wording.

Findings:

- **PR-MED-1601** (MED, classifier correctness, `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:2443`): S3 treats an unmeasured guard G as a negative test. If a text opener aligns to a still-interrogative audio segment that lost its routing cue, the audio router can drop it without recording G. S3 then falls through even when the last sentence no longer routes to the opener’s section. - Evidence: `"guard:0" in (view.segments[q]["reason"]["window"] or "")`. The input shape occurs at `.cursor/loops/ffl-out/trace/heldout-2/h2-04.json:798–805`: `"interrogative": true`, `"code": "dropped"`, `"window": null`. This demonstrates the missing diagnostic, without establishing a class change for `h2-04/f6`. Recommendation: Fix-now - Evaluate G against the text opener’s section for this case, or record the test as unmeasured and surface the affected IDs before assigning a later cause.
- **PR-LOW-1601** (LOW, failure-row integrity, `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:2516`): A duplicate non-seed fact can enter the map twice and pass every population check. Duplicating the R1-excused `syn-22/f2` would add a second `unknown` row without changing the counted failures. - Evidence: `for fact in trace["facts"]:` emits both copies, while line 2593 checks `counted = [r for r in rows if r.counted == "yes"]`. Both copies also contribute to the ranked sum. Recommendation: Fix-now - Reject repeated fact indices within an encounter before classification, preserving separate predicates for a single fact.
- **PR-LOW-1602** (LOW, archive reconciliation, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure.py:1744`): Duplicate archive rows for the same encounter and credit collapse before the new exactly-one-seed check. Two conflicting QT states can therefore publish whichever row appeared last. - Evidence: `return {(r["encounter"], r["credit"]): r for r in rows}`. Line 2877 checks `len(archived_all) == 1` only within the surviving row. Recommendation: Fix-now - Reject repeated `(encounter, credit)` keys while indexing the archive.
- **PR-LOW-1603** (LOW, set population integrity, `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:2663`): The set guard accepts duplicate residue members. For example, repeating `h6-10` in both slots of the recorded heldout-6 R3 row still passes the 2/1 check, although shipped R3 counts distinct encounters. - Evidence: `if total != len(count["members"]):` checks list length. The actual members are `h6-10 h6-22` at `.cursor/loops/ffl-out/map-3.md:113`, and `validation.py:2999` specifies “R3 counts DISTINCT encounters”. Recommendation: Fix-now - Check distinct encounter IDs for R3 and distinct encounter/fact pairs for R1 and R2 before accepting their totals.
- **PR-LOW-1604** (LOW, audio seed reporting, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure.py:2492`): An incomplete text projection can turn an unmeasured seed into `not failing`. A duplicate encounter also silently selects the first copy. The current 12 reported lines agree with the independent text output. - Evidence: `return next(e for e in entries if e["encounter"] == encounter)` followed by `silent = index in projection_of("base")["costs"]["silent"]`. The projection already carries indexed fact states at lines 1180–1183. Recommendation: Fix-now - Require one matching encounter, variant and seed fact in each projection, and count missing or ambiguous states as publication-blocking problems.
- **PR-LOW-1605** (LOW, classifier documentation, `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:2154`): The interpretation cited by the published map still describes the old segment population for W6, W4, W1, N3, P4 and P2. - Evidence: “read the fact segments the gate note quotes” and “The other segment tests read every segment” conflict with the corrected implementation at line 2343, `s in covered`, evaluated `for s in segments`. Task 3.1 repeats the old wording at `.cursor/plans/plan-follow-on-lines.md:1301`. Recommendation: Fix-now - State in both places that every fact segment participates and an unquoted segment contributes false, yielding `mixed` when the other segments satisfy the test.
- **PR-LOW-1606** (LOW, causal attribution, `.cursor/plans/plan-follow-on-lines.md:1324`): Task 3.2 attributes loss of the A1 chain to the third piece of `h2-07` L3. The second piece had already closed it. The F-credit-only result for f4 and f5 is correct. - Evidence: The plan says “the dropped third piece of line 3 cuts the chain”. The trace records s6 as `"code": "carried"` at `h2-07.json:712` and s7 as `"test": "hop:closed carry:no-chain follow:no-chain"` at line 775. The carry sets `prev_cued = False` at `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:504`. Recommendation: Fix-now - Record the sequence as s5 cue-routed, s6 carried and consumed the chain, s7 already had no chain, then s8 and s9 had no follow-on route.
- **PR-LOW-1607** (LOW, output error handling, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure.py:1160`): Both modes accept a new output name whose parent directory is missing, finish the measurement, then raise an uncaught `FileNotFoundError` during publication. - Evidence: `tempfile.mkstemp(dir=out.parent, prefix=out.name + ".", suffix=".part")` runs after the report. The preflight at lines 3025 and 3040 checks only `args.out is None or args.out.exists()`. Recommendation: Fix-now - Refuse a missing or non-directory parent before measurement and report publication-time `OSError` through the CLI’s normal failure path.

#### LEG 1 verified tuples (executor leg cx7, 2026-11-19; the peer's labels kept above)
- PR-MED-1601: materiality=behavioral severity=MED surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed. S3 at audio counts an opener piece as failing G only when its reason's `window` reads `guard:0`. A piece that is interrogative but was not cue-routed or carried has `window` null (the router never reached the window tests), so it counts as false. S3 then reads `no`, and a later cause (S8, S9) or `unknown` is assigned with no mark. The shape is real: `h2-04` s13 is interrogative, `dropped`, `window` null (`trace/heldout-2/h2-04.json:798-805`) [corrected 2026-11-19, round 26 PR-LOW-1706: those lines are segment 6's (`"index": 6` at line 737); segment 13, the S2 evidence for `h2-04/f6`, is not interrogative]. G cannot be evaluated from the trace (it would need the router run on that segment's last sentence), so the fix is the peer's second option. Such a piece is unmeasured; S3 reads `unmeasured` when no other piece fails it; the row names `S3(unmeasured)` among its marks and is listed for Task 5.3 (C6). No class can change, because S3 was not `yes` before either. → Decision: Applied (leg cx7) — `silent_tests`' S3 builds per-piece True / False / None (None when an interrogative piece has no `window`) and reads `unmeasured` when None occurs and no known piece is False. `_first` lists it as `S3(unmeasured)` beside the mixed tests, and the map's column and section are renamed "mixed or unmeasured". - PR-LOW-1601: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed: `classify_trace` (`ffl_lib.py:2516`) iterates `trace["facts"]` without checking that indices are distinct, so a repeated fact would give two rows and pass the consistency check if excused. The traces come from `build_trace` (one entry per fact), so this is a guard. Fix: a repeated index is a consistency problem. → Decision: Applied (leg cx7) — `classify_trace` adds `repeated fact index f<i>`; toy `repeated-fact`; verified by: owed (`ffl-selftest-24.txt`)
- PR-LOW-1602: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed: `load_archive` (`ffl-measure.py:1744`) builds the dict by comprehension, so a repeated `(encounter, credit)` keeps the last row silently. - PR-LOW-1603: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed: `set_level_problems` compares `total` with the members' list length only. ### Round 26 — 2026-11-19

Source: Codex — codex gpt-6.2-astra medium — Phase 3 code peer review (stage-3.p1, peer round 3)  
Status: Closed (7 applied in leg cx8, verify-then-fix; verified 2026-11-19 by the composer's re-runs `ffl-selftest-25.txt` (0 mismatches; against `ffl-selftest-24.txt` only `seconds:` differs; no `ffl-write-new-check-*` folder left), `ffl-ruff-13.txt` (all checks passed), `ffl-map-5.txt` (exit 0, `MAP problems: 0`, `[written] map-5.md`, no `.part` file left; `map-5.md` byte-equal to `map-4.md` and the log equal but for the `[written]` name, so no row changed class under PR-MED-1701 and `h6-21/f4` stays the one S3 row) and `ffl-audio-3.txt` (exit 0, errors 0; `docs-summary-3.md` equal to `docs-summary-2.md` but for the take-pair header and the timing line, the 12 seed lines identical); `map.md` to `map-4.md`, `docs-summary.md` and `docs-summary-2.md` unchanged (SHA-256 before and after))

Scope:

- job 0: checked all eight round-25 fixes against the code and saved outputs. The S3 toy reaches `S9` with an `S3(unmeasured)` mark. No recorded row gains that mark. Map logs 3 and 4 differ only at lines 19, 160 and 195, covering two headings and the published filename. Repeated fact indices, archive keys and identical residue tuples reach their refusal branches. Missing or duplicate base seed states block publication. The two missing-parent smokes reach the intended refusals. The revised origin-test wording and `h2-07` chain account agree with the code and trace. Residual cases are listed below.
- job 1: checked D5’s test order, segment and judgement predicates, window walk, and first-match selection. `h2-04/f6` is S2: its patient segment carries a label with 9.080 seconds of partner speech and 8.248 seconds of patient speech. `syn-22/f2` is `unknown`: the fact has no aligned segment, while other words on L4 survive in s8 and s9, so S6 does not hold. The carried-question case below can give S3 a false positive.
- job 2: checked the map against the baseline, shipped `class_counts`, rule v6 and both QT archives. The 67 rows comprise 13 silent, 16 wrong, 13 wrongly present, 18 encounter-code and 7 set-level rows. The ranked classes sum to 67, with 33 counted. Seven ceiling breaches span six sets. All 12 archived silent seeds and the wrong `h6-09/f1` retain their five-field states on take 1.
- job 3: checked the base and eight variants over 183 take-1 and 44 take-2 encounters, with suggestions and prose off. The D8 deltas use the recorded zero floors. Both key sets have zero base fact differences between takes. All 12 published seed lines agree with the text output and audio tables. On both takes A1 reaches `h2-04/f6`, F-credit reaches four seeds, and the rejected turn form adds `h6-16/f5` beyond A1.
- job 4: checked the seven refusal logs, both publication sites, the text defaults and the saved self-test and lint results. The final pathname is linked only after the complete write closes. An existing destination survives a competing publish. The text run reports zero errors and 63 projections. The byte comparison is recorded by the composer in the plan. A cleanup error after a successful link can produce an incorrect publication status.
- job 5: checked the Phase 3 task records and cx1–cx7 handoffs against the named outputs and traces. The counts, ranked classes, candidate reach and corrected `h2-07` sequence agree. The round-25 verification record assigns its S3 example to the wrong segment of `h2-04`.

Findings:

- **PR-MED-1701** (MED, classifier correctness, `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:2452`): S3 can diagnose a lost question when a carried continuation actually opened the audio reply window. The classifier tests the continuation in isolation before reading its recorded window decision. - Evidence: `if not seg["interrogative"]: values.append(True)`. The shipped router passes `(cue_words, words)` at `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note.py:2429` and tests `any(is_interrogative(reconstruct_span_text(piece)) for piece in question_words)` at line 2334. The cue can supply the question while the continuation supplies none, with the recorded pair passing both question and guard. Recommendation: Fix-now - Use the recorded `question` and `guard` outcomes when present. Add a carried-opener case where the continuation alone is non-interrogative and the window opens.
- **PR-LOW-1701** (LOW, seed reporting, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure.py:2546`): The exactly-one-state check covers the base seed only. A candidate projection with a missing or duplicate seed state can still supply a published reach verdict. - Evidence: `if sum(1 for f in base["facts"] if f[0] == index) != 1:` is followed by `index in projection_of(n)["costs"]["reached"]`. The audio path likewise checks `row.evaluated["base"].metrics` at line 2579 before reading candidate costs. Recommendation: Fix-now - Require exactly one seed fact in each candidate used for the line. Count a missing or ambiguous candidate state as a publication-blocking problem.
- **PR-LOW-1702** (LOW, map completeness, `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:2530`): A null gate/QT fact state is silently skipped even for a measured encounter. If the state of `syn-22/f2` were missing, the map could publish without its sole `unknown` row. - Evidence: `if row is None: continue`. The consistency check uses `counted = [r for r in rows if r.counted == "yes"]`, while `ffl-out/map-4.md:21` records this non-seed row as `silent | unknown | R1`. Removing it leaves the counted failures intact. Recommendation: Fix-now - Record a consistency problem for a missing fact state in a measured encounter. Preserve absent states for encounters that did not measure.
- **PR-LOW-1703** (LOW, residue membership, `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:2684`): The residue guard checks distinct tuples for every class, although R3 counts distinct encounters. It also leaves the separately reported marked-R1 members unchecked. - Evidence: `len({tuple(m) for m in count["members"]})` accepts `["h6-10", null]` and `["h6-10", 0]` as two R3 members. Line 2650 prints `count["reported_members"]` directly. Shipped `validation.py:2999` specifies “R3 counts DISTINCT encounters”, and `class_counts` emits `(outcome.encounter_id, None)` for R3. Recommendation: Fix-now - Validate R3’s encounter-only identity and reconcile counted and reported memberships with the encounter residue flags and rule-v6 split.
- **PR-LOW-1704** (LOW, publication status, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure.py:1211`): A cleanup failure after a successful hard link makes the CLI report that a complete output was never written. A retry then refuses the existing destination. - Evidence: `os.link(part, out)` precedes `return True`, but `finally: part.unlink(missing_ok=True)` can raise before the return completes. Both callers print `[not written] publishing ... failed` for that `OSError` at lines 2703 and 2985. Recommendation: Fix-now - Track publication separately from sibling cleanup. Report that the final file exists and identify the leftover `.part` if cleanup fails after linking.
- **PR-LOW-1705** (LOW, measurement label, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure.py:2503`): The take-pair table labels its first two counts “Fact differences, raw silent”, although a material verdict change alone contributes to either count. - Evidence: `ffl_lib.py:1693` tests `left is None or right is None or (left[2], left[5]) != (right[2], right[5])`. Both recorded counts are zero, so the label does not change the current comparison. Recommendation: Fix-now - Label both columns as material facts differing in verdict or raw silence and note that a fact present on only one take also counts.
- **PR-LOW-1706** (LOW, evidence locator, `.cursor/plans/plan-follow-on-lines.md:1284`): The round-25 verification record identifies s13 as the interrogative, dropped `h2-04` segment with an unmeasured guard. The cited lines belong to s6. - Evidence: The plan says “`h2-04` s13 is interrogative”. In `ffl-out/trace/heldout-2/h2-04.json`, `"index": 6` at line 737 owns `"interrogative": true` and `"window": null` at lines 798–805. The segment beginning `"index": 13` at line 1379 has `"interrogative": false`. Recommendation: Fix-now - Cite s6 for the S3 input shape. Keep s13 as the evidence for `h2-04/f6`’s S2 classification.

- PR-MED-1701: materiality=behavioral severity=MED surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed. `_window_tests` (`ffl_lib.py:399-422`) records `question` over every piece the window reads — `(cue_words, words)` for a carried piece, as the shipped router does (`note.py:2334`, `:2429`) — and `guard` over them joined. The segment's own `interrogative` flag reads that segment alone. S3 checks the segment flag first (`ffl_lib.py:2452`), so a carried opener piece that is not a question alone, whose recorded pair passed both `question` and `guard` (the window opened), reads as S3-holding. Fix (the peer's): where a piece's `window` is recorded, it holds S3 exactly when that record reads `question:0` or `guard:0`; only without a record does the segment's own flag decide (not interrogative: holds; interrogative: unmeasured, round 25). This can move a row: `h6-21/f4` is the one S3 row. → Decision: Applied (leg cx8) — `silent_tests`' S3 reads the recorded `window` first; the header comment and Task 3.1's interpretation line bracketed; toy `S3-carried-opener` (expected S9, unmarked); verified by: owed (`ffl-selftest-25.txt`, `map-5.md`)
- PR-LOW-1701: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed: `text_reach` and `seed_lines` check exactly one seed state in the BASE only, then read each candidate's `reached` without checking that candidate measured the fact. Fix: exactly one state of the seed in each candidate's projection `facts` (text) and in each candidate's `evaluated[name].metrics.facts` (audio), else a problem. → Decision: Applied (leg cx8) — `text_reach` checks base and every candidate projection, and `seed_lines` checks base and every candidate's metrics; the failing branches are exercised by reading only; verified by: owed (`docs-summary-3.md`'s 12 lines unchanged)
- PR-LOW-1702: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed: `classify_trace` skips a fact whose gate `QT` state is null (`if row is None: continue`) even when the encounter measured; an excused row (like `syn-22/f2`'s R1) would then vanish with no consistency problem. Fix: a null state on a measured encounter is a problem; an unmeasured encounter keeps its null states. → Decision: Applied (leg cx8) — `classify_trace` adds `measured but no gate state for f<i>`; the failing branch is exercised by reading only; verified by: owed (`map-5.md`, `MAP problems: 0`)
- PR-LOW-1703: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed: the round-25 check compares distinct tuples, so `[e, null]` and `[e, 0]` would count as two R3 members, and `reported_members` is not checked at all. The traces hold everything needed to rebuild the shipped memberships:
  - R2 is the facts with `r2`.
  - R3 is one `(encounter, null)` per encounter with any `r3` fact (`EncounterMetrics.r3_event`, `validation.py:1228-1231`).
  - R1, on a row that reports marked R1 (`r1_marked_reported`), is the `r1` facts that are not marked-wrong (`residue_r1_marked` = `r1` and verdict `wrong` and not raw-silent, `validation.py:1003-1006`), and `reported_members` is those marked-wrong facts. On every other row R1 is all `r1` facts and nothing is reported.

  All of these are counted over measured encounters only (`class_counts` skips `metrics is None`). Fix: rebuild each set's expected members and reported members from its traces and require `_set.json`'s to equal them as sorted lists. This subsumes round 25's distinct-member check. → Decision: Applied (leg cx8) — `ffl_lib.set_membership_problems`, called by `map_report` per set with that set's traces and the rule row's `r1_marked_reported`; round 25's check is kept (cheap, now redundant). Self-test: a marked-wrong R1 fact reported on a split row, counted on an unsplit one (2 problems), and an R3 member with no `r3` fact (1 problem); verified by: owed (`ffl-selftest-25.txt`, `map-5.md`)
- PR-LOW-1704: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed: in `write_new`, the `finally`'s `part.unlink` can raise after a successful `os.link`, and both callers then print `[not written]` although the complete file exists. Fix: publishing is tracked apart from cleanup. A cleanup failure after the link raises a dedicated `LeftoverPartError`, and the callers report `[written] <name>` and `[leftover] <part> not removed (<type>)`, exit 1. A failure before the link keeps the `[not written]` path. → Decision: Applied (leg cx8) — `write_new` tracks `published` and raises `LeftoverPartError` only after a link. The two sites' identical reporting moved into one `publish_report` (a pattern sibling, so the two cannot drift). Self-test: `Path.unlink` patched to fail for one write (restored in `finally`), which must raise `LeftoverPartError` with the file whole; verified by: owed (`ffl-selftest-25.txt`)
- PR-LOW-1705: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — confirmed: `take_pair_lines`' two headers say "Fact differences, raw silent", but `lib.fact_floor` (`ffl_lib.py:1688-1695`) counts a material fact whose verdict OR raw silent flag differs, or which is on one take only. Both recorded values are 0, so only the label changes. → Decision: Applied (leg cx8) — the two headers now read "Material facts differing in verdict or raw silence (reply credit; one take only counts)" and "The same (no reply credit)"; verified by: owed (`docs-summary-3.md`: that header line is its one expected text change)
- PR-LOW-1706: materiality=docs-only severity=LOW surface=docs rec=Fix-now — confirmed: in `trace/heldout-2/h2-04.json` the block holding `"interrogative": true` and `"window": null` at lines 798-805 is segment 6 (`"index": 6` at line 737; segment 7 starts at line 833), while segment 13 starts at line 1379. Smoke cases (a) to (h) show every refusal an agent shell can reach.
- The print surface is ordinals and integers only. The row is `| n | yes |` with eleven integers, or `| n | no |` with eleven dashes; the aggregate prints counts, `COMPLETE` / `INCOMPLETE`, and type names through `_error_type` (type name or `Exception`). No session id, path, word, label or name is printed. Neither the folder nor the enrolment path is echoed in a refusal; only `tool_wer_refusal_line` names the interpreter, as the replay tool does. The grep over both dry runs printed nothing.
- Custody is `afq-measure-kept.py`'s. The audio is read into memory per recording only (`pcm` deleted in `finally`). Each recording is re-transcribed into a memory-keyed store (`persist_key=False`), and the stored transcript, saved note, label and generated note are never read. A store not shown gone stops the run (`_Custody`), so does an interrupt (one fixed line, no aggregate), and an unhandled exception is reported by type only after the handler.
- D9's counts are computed as written. "Whether the clinician was enrolled" is the row's `yes` / `no`. Clinician lines, questions and cue-routed questions are read from the base request, as the router sees it. Windows opened are the shipped `_opens_answer_window` decisions the reasons record (`cued`/`window`, `carried`/`…+window`). Hop joined is the base's `joined`. Replies flagged is the chooser's `replies_after` call. A1 and A1s follows are each variant's `followed`. The guard columns are G0's and A1+guard's `refused`. F's extra marks are D8's H7 definition. The dry run's five cross-checks equal Task 3.2's take-1 audio tables exactly (42 / 32 / 0 / 0 / 57), so the shared per-document function agrees with the method of record on the same documents.
- The per-document premise compares the scratch base note with the SHIPPED provider's under the pass's own config, and each composition's trace is held to Premise 1's test, so a config in which the scratch drifts makes that recording fail, never a quiet number. The second dry-run pass shows the premise holding under a second config folder that carries prefill templates.
- C3: the script shares no printing code with `ffl-measure.py`, and `_trace_ok` is a deliberate copy (`ffl-measure.py` cannot be imported). The dry run accepts only the seven sets' frozen ids, so it cannot be pointed at an archive. `COMPLETE` is `afq-measure-kept.py`'s rule (expected above 0, none failed, every one measured), with exit 0 only then.
- Interpretation (7), `--enrolment` required, changes nothing that is measured: without it every D9 count is 0 by construction (A1, the hop, F and the reply flag all need a confirmed clinician). Task 4.2's two passes name the practitioner's `enrolment\me.wav`. [Corrected 2026-11-19, peer round 30 PR-MED-2101: the shipped carry has no clinician test, so the guard columns are not 0 by construction. Without a confirmed clinician they are simply not measured, because an unenrolled recording is not composed. The conclusion stands: requiring `--enrolment` changes nothing that is measured.]
Missed-issue pass: re-read `ffl-measure-kept.py` in full and `ffl_lib.py:308-664` (the provider, its window tests, the variants and F); result: none.
Finding verification: 0 candidates.
Cap verdict: none needed (converged, round 1 of 3). No re-run is owed.

### Round 30 — 2026-11-19
Source: Codex — codex gpt-6.2-astra medium — Phase 4 code peer review (stage-4.p1, peer round 1)  
Status: Closed (5 verified LOW in leg dx3, Fix-now, applied there (verify-then-fix) and disposed by the composer; verified 2026-11-19 by the composer's re-runs `ffl-ruff-17.txt` (all checks passed), `ffl-kept-dry-3.txt` and `ffl-kept-dry-4.txt` (exit 0, 183 / 183, `COMPLETE`; against `ffl-kept-dry-1.txt` / `-2.txt` exactly the two added lines, the `[config]` line and the not-enrolled line, every row and total byte-equal; the numbers-only grep printed nothing on both), `ffl-kept-smoke-3.txt` (equal to `ffl-kept-smoke-1.txt`; no `no-such-config` folder created) and `ffl-kept-smoke-4.txt` (`--config protocol`: the `[config]` line reads no for all four files, exit 0, `COMPLETE`, totals equal to `ffl-kept-dry-4.txt`'s, so a wrong folder is visible only on that line); `ffl_lib.py` and `ffl-measure.py` unchanged (SHA-256 before and after); peer round 2 of 5 next)

Scope:

- job 1: checked archive enumeration, decryption, refusal order, output and cleanup. The measuring path checks the folder and entry keys, checks Windows Error Reporting, then holds the instance lock through measurement. It calls `read_recording` only. The stored transcript, notes and name label stay encrypted. Its explicit row and error printers use ordinals, counts, fixed text and exception types. Two custody details need correction below.
- job 2: checked all eleven columns against D6, D7 and D9. For enrolled recordings, the clinician-line filter, question test, cue and window reasons, joined lines, shipped reply flags, A1/A1s follows, both guard counts and F’s additional marks match their definitions. The guard can also act on an unenrolled recording. An existing but unintended config folder can silently select shipped cues.
- job 3: checked the equality test, trace validation, enrollment counts and `COMPLETE`. For each enrolled recording, both providers receive the same document, clinician, config and fixed time, and the comparison covers the generated note. A load or composition exception makes the pass `INCOMPLETE`. An unenrolled row currently counts as measured without running either provider or either guard.
- job 4: checked both dry-run logs row by row. Each has 183 consecutive enrolled ordinals, zero failures, and eleven totals equal to the sums of its rows. The five validation-config cross-checks independently sum to **42 / 32 / 0 / 0 / 57** across the seven take-1 audio tables. Ruff reports success. The eight smoke cases comprise **seven refusals and one successful `--list`**. Case (h) reached `WavFormatError` before `[start]`; it did not exercise a held lock. The dry-run input check verifies filenames only.
- job 5: checked the practitioner path against the replay launcher, replay implementation and operator guide. `--list` counts kept entries without decrypting them. The measuring run checks the actual interpreter and acquires the shared lock on the practitioner’s machine. Task 4.2’s PowerShell commands and hash checks are still pending as recorded in leg dx2. The saved Git Bash smoke establishes neither the practitioner’s Windows Error Reporting state nor lock contention on that terminal.

Findings:

- **PR-HIGH-2101** (HIGH, dry-run input custody, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure-kept.py:515`): A different folder containing the expected filenames passes the synthetic-take check. Its contents then reach unrestricted pickle deserialization before transcript validation. A substituted file can execute code with the practitioner’s permissions. - Evidence: `if tuple(p.stem for p in found) != lib.EXPECTED_IDS[name]:` is the acceptance check. The loader at `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:757` executes `return pickle.loads(path.read_bytes())`. Recommendation: Fix-now - Verify each file’s bytes against a trusted take-1 digest before deserializing those same bytes, or use a non-executable transcript format with equivalent provenance checks.
- **PR-MED-2101** (MED, measurement correctness, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure-kept.py:579`): Skipping composition for an unenrolled recording loses real G0 and A1+guard refusals. The shipped same-speaker carry can operate without a confirmed clinician. If an otherwise carryable line contains a D7 phrase, both guards would refuse it. A pass containing only unenrolled recordings can therefore report `COMPLETE` with both guard totals zero despite having measured neither. - Evidence: `None if clinician is None else measure_document(document, clinician, config)` skips the recording’s routing. At `.cursor/loops/ffl_lib.py:481`, `if refused and (hop is None or carry is None or follow_on):` includes the carry independently. `_carry_fails` at line 345 takes no clinician argument. Recommendation: Fix-now - Measure the guard paths under the actual unconfirmed-clinician state, preserve the enrollment count separately, and check this case with a synthetic carry before freezing.
- **PR-MED-2102** (MED, config verification, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure-kept.py:394`): Passing an existing directory one level above the intended config can produce `COMPLETE` under shipped defaults. Both providers receive that same fallback config, so their equality check cannot detect the wrong pass. - Evidence: `if not config_dir.is_dir():` is the only folder-content precondition. The function documents that “a missing file inside an existing folder stays the loader's ordinary default.” D9 specifies “the validation cue file, then the installed app's own.” Recommendation: Fix-now - Verify the intended cue source for each named pass before decryption. If the installed pass legitimately uses shipped cues, identify that outcome with a fixed status line.
- **PR-LOW-2101** (LOW, in-memory custody, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure-kept.py:581`): A composition failure leaves the failed recording’s transcript referenced while the next recording is decrypted and transcribed. `del document` runs only after successful composition, and the next `load()` runs before assignment replaces the old reference. - Evidence: `document, clinician = load()` precedes `measure_document(document, clinician, config)` and `del document`. The failure handler then reaches `continue` at line 590. Recommendation: Fix-now - Scope loading and measurement to a per-recording function, or release the document in `finally` before advancing to the next ordinal.
- **PR-LOW-2102** (LOW, custody documentation, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure-kept.py:32`): The script’s statement that nothing is written anywhere gives the practitioner an incorrect account of temporary disk use. The replay design writes encrypted audio under `%TEMP%` and keeps its key in memory. - Evidence: “Nothing is written anywhere” conflicts with `docs/testing/kept-recordings.md:79`, which says the audio is “re-encrypted into a temporary store under `%TEMP%\scribe-speaker-eval-*`”. The script invokes that path with `persist_key=False` at line 484. Recommendation: Fix-now - Describe the archive as read-only and state where temporary ciphertext is written, how the key is held, and when cleanup occurs.

Verification: 15 scoped files read, 5 of 5 jobs evidenced. The review used static inspection and arithmetic over the supplied logs. No scripts ran, no files were written, and no practitioner data was accessed.

PEER-ROUND-30 RESULT: 5 findings (CRIT 0 / HIGH 1 / MED 2 / LOW 2).

#### LEG 1 verified tuples (executor leg dx3, 2026-11-19; the peer's labels kept above)
- PR-HIGH-2101: materiality=docs-only severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now (state the trust boundary; the proposed digest check is not taken) — The claim is true as mechanics: `_dry_run` checks file names only, and `ffl_lib.load_pickle` is `pickle.loads`. But it opens no new exposure. `--dry-run-docs` is a developer check the composer points at `C:/ffl-sets/docs`, the folder its own chain wrote (Task 2.1). Task 4.2's commands never use it, and the measuring run reads no pickle. Every Phase 2–3 mode of `ffl-measure.py` (`--premise2`, `--baseline`, `--docs`, `--audio`) already unpickles the same files. Anyone able to plant a file there can equally edit the script, `ffl_lib.py` or the venv. So a digest list would add a trust anchor no stronger than the files around it. The fix states the boundary in the script's docstring and on Task 4.1.
- PR-MED-2101: materiality=docs-only severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now (correct two over-claims; the behaviour is kept) — The mechanics are true: `FollowProvider._carry_fails` (`ffl_lib.py:345-368`) has no clinician test, so on an unenrolled recording the shipped carry can place a line and G0 / A1+guard could refuse one. Two statements are therefore wrong: the script's docstring ("no rule of 0.3.7 or of the candidates acts without a confirmed clinician") and Task 4.1 interpretation (7) and Round 29 ("every D9 count is 0 by construction"). The harm claimed does not follow. D9 describes where the rules act on the recordings, and the hop, A1, A1s, F and the reply flag all need a confirmed clinician. The harness composes only with one: `_compose_note` takes `clinician: str`, and an unresolved role is `role_unresolved` and never composed. The aggregate is labelled "totals over enrolled recordings" and prints `n enrolled`, and Task 4.2 records that count. So an all-unenrolled pass reads `n enrolled: 0` beside its totals, never a measured zero. The proposed fix, composing unenrolled recordings with no clinician, would measure outside the harness's method and is not taken. The fix corrects the docstring, interpretation (7) and Round 29 with dated brackets, and has the aggregate name that guard refusals on unenrolled recordings are not measured.
- PR-MED-2102: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now (the proposed fix's second form: a fixed status line) — True. `_config_dir` checks only `is_dir`, and `load_note_config` (`note_config.py:1009-1040`) reads each of its four files, absent ones as the shipped default, so an existing wrong folder gives the shipped config and the premise cannot see it. LOW, not MED: Task 4.2's two passes name fixed, copy-pasted folders (`validation\config`, `"$env:LOCALAPPDATA\ClinikoScribe\config"`), so the case needs a different path typed by hand. Refusing a folder without `section_cues.json` would be wrong, because the installed folder may legitimately lack a file. The fix prints one fixed line before `[start]` naming which of the four files the folder holds (`yes` / `no`, file names only, no content), in both the measuring run and the dry run, so the record shows what each pass read.
- PR-LOW-2101: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — True. In `_measure_all`, `del document` runs only after a successful `measure_document`. On a failure the loop continues with `document` still bound to the failed recording's transcript while the next `load()` decrypts and transcribes. The fix releases the reference in a `finally` before the next ordinal.
- PR-LOW-2102: materiality=docs-only severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — True. "Nothing is written anywhere" contradicts the same sentence's temporary store and `docs/testing/kept-recordings.md:79` (audio re-encrypted under `%TEMP%\scribe-speaker-eval-*`, its key never on disk, removed key-first after each entry). The fix rewords it to: nothing written to the archive, the repository or any report, and the temporary ciphertext, where it sits, how its key is held and when it goes.
Cap verdict: accept — test-harness — round 1 of 5; all five verify LOW, Fix-now, inside the git-ignored scratch and this plan's records; none is a regression, a scope expansion or production-impacting; nothing deferred. The composer's re-runs verify the fixes and the next peer round confirms them.
Leg route: verify-then-fix

Final dispositions (leg dx3, `/fix`, one at a time; verification by the composer's re-runs owed):
- PR-HIGH-2101 → LOW, Applied: the script's docstring states the dry run's trust boundary, and Task 4.1's interpretation (8) is made precise with a dated bracket. The behaviour is unchanged.
- PR-MED-2101 → LOW, Applied: the docstring says the carry, and so the guard's refusal of a carried line, needs no clinician and is not measured on an unenrolled recording. The aggregate prints a fixed line, `- not enrolled: not composed, so not measured (the carry and the guard included)`, and interpretation (7) and Round 29's bullet are corrected with dated brackets. Unenrolled recordings are still not composed.
- PR-MED-2102 → LOW, Applied: `_load_config` prints `config_files_line`, `[config] files in the --config folder: <name> yes|no, …`, naming each of the four files `load_note_config` reads (`CONFIG_FILENAMES`), after the config loads and before `[start]`, in both the measuring run and the dry run.
- PR-LOW-2101 → Applied: `_measure_all` binds `document` to None before each load and releases it in a `finally`, so a failed recording's transcript is never held while the next is decrypted.
- PR-LOW-2102 → Applied: the docstring's custody sentence now says the archive is only read and nothing is written to it, the repository or any report. It names the `%TEMP%` store (`scribe-speaker-eval-*`), says its key is held in memory only (`persist_key=False`), that it is removed key-first when the recording's transcription ends, and that a store not shown gone stops the run.
Fix notes: the only behavioural changes are the two new fixed lines (`[config]` before `[start]`, the aggregate's not-enrolled line) and the `finally`. Rows, totals, `COMPLETE` and the refusals are unchanged. `ffl_lib.py` is untouched, so the self-test and both premises stand. The fixes need the re-runs in leg dx3's handoff bullet.
Fix-delta self-check: PASS — re-read every hunk.
- The config line is computed only after `load_note_config` succeeds. The refusal path still prints only `[refused] --config unreadable (<type>)`. The line holds four fixed file names and `yes` / `no`, with no path or content.
- In the measuring run the line prints after the lock and offline checks and before the models load and the listing, so before anything is decrypted. In the dry run it prints after the inventory check, before `[dry-run] …`.
- `document = None` in the `finally` runs on success, on a counted failure and before `_Custody` propagates. `measure` holds integers only.
- The new aggregate line is fixed text.
- No new backslash, CR or NUL in the file. The only backslash is the existing `\n` in the parser's refusal.

#### LEG 1 verified tuples (executor leg dx6, 2026-11-19; the peer's labels kept above)
- PR-MED-2401: materiality=behavioral severity=MED surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now (the proposed preflight, metadata only, shared by `--list` and the measuring run, before the first decryption) — True. `kept_session_ids` → `_committed_dirs` (`past_sessions.py:1702-1705`) yields an entry only when `not _exists(entry / PENDING_FILENAME)`. `_exists` (`:2082-2091`) reads a stat error other than absence as present, so an entry whose pending marker, link status (`_is_link`: unreadable counts as a link) or folder status cannot be read is silently skipped. The shipped replay has the same property (`replay_kept._run`: `sorted(store.kept_session_ids())`, exit 0 with rows and no error); the shipped direction is right for the app, which must never commit through a doubt. But this script's `COMPLETE` is D9's acceptance test for a pass, and only totals enter the plan, so a short denominator would be invisible there. MED kept. An unreadable audio key does NOT hide an entry (`_recording_state`: `unknown` with `audio.enc` present reads `kept`, so the later `read_recording` fails and is counted). The fix: a preflight over the session-id-named children refuses the run (or `--list`) when any child's link status, folder status, key-file status or pending-marker status cannot be read. A confirmed link, a non-folder, a confirmed pending entry and a folder without a key are still skipped as the shipped listing skips them. It is metadata only, run before the listing; nothing is decrypted.
- PR-LOW-2401: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — True. The `scribe_desktop` and `ffl_lib` imports run at module scope, before `main`'s type-only handler, so a missing dependency prints Python's traceback with local paths (no recording open; a developer machine). The fix wraps the imports: a failure prints one fixed line with the exception's type name and exits 2, with no traceback.
- PR-LOW-2402: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — True. `_aggregate` prints `- status: COMPLETE` before `n enrolled` and the eleven totals, so an interruption while printing could leave `COMPLETE` above a partial block. The fix prints the status as the block's last line. The expected outputs change only by that line's position.
- PR-LOW-2403: materiality=docs-only severity=LOW surface=docs rec=Fix-now — True. Leg dx5's bracket on leg dx1 says the no-file claim "holds for these dry runs and smokes only", but those runs also import the scratch and may write the code cache. The docstring's "the one copy of a recording on disk is the temporary ciphertext" omits the archive's own encrypted copy. The fix rewords both: an ADDITIONAL temporary encrypted copy, and the code cache on every run.

Findings:

- **PR-MED-2501** (MED, measurement completeness, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure-kept.py:448`): A metadata error on the second scan can still produce a short `COMPLETE`. For example, if a committed recording’s pending-marker check succeeds during preflight but raises `PermissionError` during the shipped listing, the listing silently omits that recording. One other readable recording is enough for the pass to report one expected, one measured and `COMPLETE`. - Evidence: `unclear = indeterminate_entries(folder)` is followed by `kept = replay_kept._open_store(folder).kept_session_ids()`. The second scan requires `not _exists(entry / PENDING_FILENAME)` at `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:1704`, and `_exists` returns `True` on `OSError` at line 2089. Recommendation: Fix-now - Build the list used for `expected` from the same strict metadata decisions that establish eligibility. Add a synthetic check where only the second marker read fails.
- **PR-LOW-2501** (LOW, unnecessary refusal, `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure-kept.py:432`): A confirmed pending entry with unreadable key metadata blocks both listing and measurement, although its pending marker already proves it is ineligible. - Evidence: `if key == "unknown" or _stat_state(entry / PENDING_FILENAME) == "unknown":` short-circuits before reading the marker when the key status is unknown. The round-33 disposition at `.cursor/plans/plan-follow-on-lines.md:1741` explicitly says confirmed pending entries are skipped. Recommendation: Fix-now - Read the marker when key status is unknown, skip a confirmed pending entry, and cover that combination in the synthetic check.
- **PR-LOW-2502** (LOW, verification record, `.cursor/plans/plan-follow-on-lines.md:1751`): The round-33 self-check makes an incorrect claim about both source files. - Evidence: “No backslash, CR or NUL in either file.” The parser refusal contains `see --help\n` at `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure-kept.py:352`, and the new check expects `"kept recordings: 0\n"` at `.cursor/loops/ffl-kept-check.py:88`. Recommendation: Fix-now - Correct the plan record to distinguish the intentional newline escapes from any file-level CR or NUL check.

#### LEG 1 verified tuples (executor leg dx8, 2026-11-19; the peer's labels kept above)
- PR-MED-2501: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — True as stated, verified LOW. `_kept_listing` (`ffl-measure-kept.py:446-448`) runs `indeterminate_entries` and then `kept_session_ids()`, which re-reads all of the same metadata in its own scan: `_entry_dirs` (`past_sessions.py:1699`: `_is_link` unreadable reads as a link, and `child.is_dir()` reads an error as not a folder) and `_committed_dirs` (`:1704`: `_exists` at `:2089` reads an error on `pending` as present). An error that appears only on the second read therefore drops an entry silently, and `expected` falls with it. The other reads cannot drop an entry: `_exists(key)` reads an error as present, and `_recording_state` (`:1877-1896`) gives `none` only on a CONFIRMED absent or zeroed audio key (`_audio_key_state`, `:1852-1871`: `unknown` falls through to `_exists(audio.enc)`, which reads an error as kept, so a later `read_recording` failure counts against `COMPLETE`). The verified severity is LOW, not MED. The gap needs an error that is absent at the preflight and present milliseconds later on the same file, with both builds closed under the instance lock and the folder written only by this Windows login; every persistent error is already refused (round 33's fix, proved by `ffl-kept-check-2.txt`). The consequence is the one round 33 named, a short denominator under `COMPLETE` in a totals-only record. The proposed fix is sound and cheap before the freeze, and it closes the class by construction. The preflight becomes the listing: one strict scan decides each entry ONCE and returns the eligible ids (doubt → refuse). The kept list is those ids for which the shipped `past_sessions._recording_kept_in` holds, and it is cross-checked equal to `kept_session_ids()`. Any difference refuses with a fixed line, so the shipped listing is still the list on the normal path and a second-read drop cannot pass unseen. Synthetic check: a `pending` stat that fails on its SECOND call only. That case needs kept entries, so made-up `audio.enc` (empty) and `audio-key.enc` (32 bytes of `0x01`, made-up, not key material) files, which `_audio_key_state` reads as `live`. Final: Applied (leg dx9).
- PR-LOW-2501: materiality=behavioral severity=LOW surface=test-harness rec=Fix-now — True. `ffl-measure-kept.py:432` refuses on `key == "unknown"` before it reads the marker. The shipped listing skips a confirmed pending entry whatever its key status (`:1704`: `_exists(key)` reads an error as present, then `_exists(pending)` is True), and round 33's disposition (plan line 1741) says confirmed pending entries are skipped. The refusal is fail-closed (no wrong count), so the defect is an unnecessary refusal plus a record that over-states the rule. Fold it into PR-MED-2501's scan, deciding in this order: key confirmed absent → skip; marker confirmed present → skip; key or marker unknown → doubt; otherwise eligible. Add the combination (key unknown, marker present → skipped, preflight 0) to the check. Final: Applied (leg dx9).
- PR-LOW-2502: materiality=docs-only severity=LOW surface=docs rec=Fix-now — True. Round 33's self-check line (plan line 1751) says "No backslash, CR or NUL in either file". A content search finds exactly three backslashes, all `\n` string escapes: `ffl-measure-kept.py:352`, and `ffl-kept-check.py:88` and `:100`. It finds no CR and no NUL in `ffl-measure-kept.py`, `ffl-kept-check.py` or `ffl_lib.py`, and no backslash path. The fix appends a dated bracket that keeps the line and states what holds: no CR or NUL, no backslash path, and the three `\n` escapes intended. Final: Applied (leg dx9).
Cap verdict: accept — test-harness — round 5 of 5 (the cap); findings by round 5, 3, 5, 4, 3, each round's set confirmed fixed by the next (this round's job 6 confirms all four of round 33's). The pass is converging: the later findings narrow from behaviour to millisecond windows between two metadata reads and to record wording. None is a regression, a scope expansion or production-impacting (git-ignored scratch and this plan only), and the verified severities are LOW, LOW, LOW. The three Fix-now edits are confined to the listing preflight, the check file and one plan bracket. They change no count, row or `COMPLETE` rule on the normal path, and they are verifiable without another peer round: the synthetic check gains the second-read and pending-with-unknown-key cases, the dry runs and smokes must be byte-equal to `-9` / `-10`, and lint must be clean. - PR-MED-2501 → Applied: `indeterminate_entries` becomes `committed_entries(folder) -> (committed ids, doubt count)`. It is one metadata scan that decides each session-id-named child once, in the order of PR-LOW-2501's tuple. `_kept_listing` builds the kept list from those decisions only, applying the shipped `past_sessions._recording_kept_in` to each committed entry; that is the key file's zero-check, the same read `kept_session_ids` makes, and no decryption. It then reads the shipped `kept_session_ids()` and refuses on any difference with one fixed line: `[refused] the kept recordings were listed differently on a second read, so they cannot be counted; nothing was decrypted` (exit 2). Its structure is `afq-measure-kept.py`'s: `--list` (decrypts nothing); `--enrolment` and `--config` both required, the config an existing folder; `replay_kept.main`'s refusal order (dev channel, enrolment, config, folder, entries, the crash-report exclusion, the single-instance lock held to the end, the offline environment, models, config, enrolment WAV, listing); ordinals oldest first; each recording re-transcribed in memory (`persist_key=False`), never the stored transcript; by-type errors; custody and interrupt stops; one aggregate block with `COMPLETE` (expected above 0, none failed, every one measured) and exit 0 only then. The per-document function `measure_document` (shared by the measuring run and `--dry-run-docs`) composes the base through the scratch router and checks it against the SHIPPED provider's note under the same config (`PremiseMismatchError`), checks each composition's trace (`TraceMismatchError`), then counts, per enrolled recording, D9's eleven columns: clinician lines; questions; cue-routed questions; windows opened; hop joined; replies flagged; A1 follows; A1s follows; guard refuses G0; guard refuses A1+guard; F extra marks. The aggregate prints totals only (C2), plus recordings expected, measured, failed by type, status and n enrolled / not enrolled. Interpretations, for the review: (1) "lines" are the base request's non-blank utterances, i.e. those before the next-patient boundary, as the router sees them; (2) "questions" are `is_interrogative` over the line's own words (the window's question test, on one piece); (3) "windows opened" counts every opening, by a cue-routed line or a carried piece (switch S), all five tests passed; (4) "replies flagged" is the shipped reply flag on the base note (`replies_after(…, added_questions=True)`, the chooser's call), not the chooser's whole mark; (5) "lines the diagnostic guard would refuse" is printed under two variants, G0 (0.3.7 plus the guard) and A1+guard, because the guard refuses only a line some path would place; (6) "extra marks from F" is D8's H7 "extra flagged" definition (`chooser_flags` under F-marks less the base's); (7) `--enrolment` stays required, as in `afq-measure-kept.py`: Task 4.2's "where there is one" is met by the practitioner's existing `enrolment\me.wav`, and without an enrolment no clinician is confirmed, so every D9 count would be 0 by construction [corrected 2026-11-19, peer round 30 PR-MED-2101: without a confirmed clinician no recording is composed (the harness composes only with one), so the totals are over 0 enrolled recordings. That is not "every count 0 by construction": the shipped carry has no clinician test, so on an unenrolled recording the guard could refuse a carried line, and that is not measured. The script and its aggregate now say so]; (8) the dry run accepts only a folder holding exactly the seven seen sets' frozen ids (`EXPECTED_IDS`), so it can read nothing else [narrowed 2026-11-19, peer round 37 PR-LOW-2602: the check is that each of the seven sets' subfolders holds `.pickle` files named exactly by its frozen ids (`ffl-measure-kept.py:720-725`); other entries at the root are not checked, and only those pickles are read] [made precise 2026-11-19, peer round 30 PR-HIGH-2101: the check is by file name, and the dry run unpickles what it is given, as every Phase 2–3 mode of `ffl-measure.py` does over the same files. It is a developer check pointed only at `C:/ffl-sets/docs`, the folder the composer's own chain wrote. The practitioner's Task 4.2 commands never use it, and the measuring run reads no pickle. The script's docstring states this boundary]; its second pass uses the repository's `docs/testing/shipping-gate-config` as a synthetic stand-in for "another config folder", because the installed app's config is under `%LOCALAPPDATA%`, which no agent opens.

    **0. Before you start:**
    - Clinic Scribe is closed, BOTH builds: the installed app and the developer build. The script refuses otherwise.

[Reworded 2026-11-19, leg dx11, after Task 4.2's first two attempts were refused that way.]

    **1. Go to the repository:**

    ```powershell
    cd "C:\Recording clinic software"
    ```

    **2. Check that the two script files are the reviewed ones.** Each command must print `True` (PowerShell's `-eq` ignores case, and `Get-FileHash` prints capitals). If either prints `False`, STOP and tell the composer.

    ```powershell
    (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 .cursor\loops\ffl-measure-kept.py).Hash -eq '36e215c5fe92e0734f7161226c99b72b0fd956235c8a8630395ccb8df25f603d'
    ```

    ```powershell
    (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 .cursor\loops\ffl_lib.py).Hash -eq 'f7d2d75c4e1dbac9ba91413ee7f42d546fc7fc0575e3ffe0b6aba43314d38d03'
    ```

    **3. Count the kept recordings** (this decrypts nothing). It must print one line, `kept recordings: N`. Each pass then takes about 15 minutes per recording (N × 15 min), and there are two passes. Paste back the line.

    ```powershell
    .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe .cursor\loops\ffl-measure-kept.py "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\ClinikoScribe\past_sessions" --list
    ```

    **4. Pass 1, with the repository's validation config:**

    ```powershell
    .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe .cursor\loops\ffl-measure-kept.py "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\ClinikoScribe\past_sessions" --enrolment "<measurement folder>\enrolment\me.wav" --config validation\config; "exit=$LASTEXITCODE"
    ```

    **5. Pass 2, with the installed app's own config:**

    ```powershell
    .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe .cursor\loops\ffl-measure-kept.py "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\ClinikoScribe\past_sessions" --enrolment "<measurement folder>\enrolment\me.wav" --config "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\ClinikoScribe\config"; "exit=$LASTEXITCODE"
    ```

    **What each pass prints, in order:**
    - one `[config] files in the --config folder: …` line (file names with `yes` / `no`);
    - `[start] recordings`;
    - a table header, then one row per recording: its ordinal, `yes` / `no` for enrolled, and eleven numbers;
    - the block that begins `## Aggregate (integers only; totals over enrolled recordings)` and ends with `- status: COMPLETE` as its LAST line;
    - then `exit=0`.

    Pass 2's `[config]` line shows which of your own config files exist; a `no` means the shipped default was read for that file.

    **6. What to paste back, per pass:**
    - the `[config]` line;
    - the block from `## Aggregate (integers only; …)` to its last line, `- status: …`;
    - the `exit=` line.

    Never paste the per-recording rows above the aggregate, and nothing else per recording. Only totals are recorded here (C2).

    **7. If something goes wrong:**
    - **The aggregate ends `- status: INCOMPLETE` (`exit=1`):** paste the block anyway (its `failed:` line names each failure by type only) and do not re-run. The cause is looked at first.
    - **A line starting `[refused]` (`exit=2`):** nothing was measured, and nothing was decrypted before the refusal. Paste that one line. It names its own remedy:
      - close Clinic Scribe;
      - check the enrolment WAV's path;
      - for crash reports not excluded for this interpreter, re-run `scripts\register-native-host.py` from a normal terminal (`AGENTS.md` run step 5);
      - for a model not installed, the setup script's remedy;
      - for entry folders that cannot be read, or kept recordings listed differently on a second read, try the pass again once; if it refuses again, stop and tell the composer.

      Then run the pass again once the cause is gone.
    - **A line starting `[custody]` or `[stopped]` (`exit=1`, no aggregate block):** paste that one line. Then look in `%TEMP%` for the `scribe-speaker-eval-*` folder it names and delete it.
    - **Any `[error] the run stopped (<type>)` line:** paste it.
